// FUNC 180032e34 __scrt_acquire_startup_lock
char _scrt_acquire_startup_lock()
{
  PVOID StackBase; // rcx
  signed __int64 v1; // rax

  if ( (unsigned int)_scrt_is_ucrt_dll_in_use() != 0 )
  {
    StackBase = NtCurrentTeb()->NtTib.StackBase;
    while ( 1 )
    {
      v1 = _InterlockedCompareExchange64(&_scrt_native_startup_lock, (signed __int64)StackBase, 0);
      if ( v1 == 0 )
        break;
      if ( StackBase == (PVOID)v1 )
        return 1;
    }
  }
  return 0;
}

// FUNC 180032e70 __scrt_dllmain_after_initialize_c
char _scrt_dllmain_after_initialize_c()
{
  _crt_argv_mode startup_argv_mode; // eax

  if ( (unsigned int)_scrt_is_ucrt_dll_in_use() != 0 )
  {
    _isa_available_init();
  }
  else
  {
    startup_argv_mode = get_startup_argv_mode();
    if ( configure_narrow_argv(startup_argv_mode) != 0 )
      return 0;
    initialize_narrow_environment();
  }
  return 1;
}

// FUNC 180032ebc __scrt_dllmain_crt_thread_attach
char _scrt_dllmain_crt_thread_attach()
{
  if ( !_acrt_thread_attach() )
    return 0;
  if ( !_acrt_thread_attach() )
  {
    _vcrt_thread_detach();
    return 0;
  }
  return 1;
}

{
